Archway of stalagmites. (indoors)
A noxious, red-hued fog overwhelms the area with a thick, palpable vapour. Wide, towering spires of rock fill the cavern with their spiked pinnacles, disappearing into the darkness as they reach toward the high ceiling. Huge, grey stalagmites loom within the murky shadows, illuminated by the muted green glow of spidery runes engraved onto their surfaces. The stone behemoths form a broad archway, the rock pillars standing like silent, eternal sentinels at the far end of the cave. Around the arched entrance small deposits of multicoloured limestone and minerals are collected, imparting an eerie sparkle to the petrified giants nearby. Echoing faintly within the gloomy shadows, the slow and steady drip of water keeps utter silence at bay. Bloated with blood and flesh, a frenzied bloodworm sets its beady eyes on you. A razor-beaked falcon looms overhead, seeking prey.
You see exits leading north, east, south, and west.
